{"success": true, "name": "acms-project-operations", "description": "Operate the ACMS control plane (startupteams/acms-project-framework): branch/PR/no-self-merge discipline, safe release + rollback tooling, live CT122 quirks, Docker/Compose pitfalls.", "tags": ["ACMS", "deployment", "rollback", "docker-compose", "jordan-workflow"], "related_skills": [], "content": "---\nname: acms-project-operations\ndescription: \"Operate the ACMS control plane (startupteams/acms-project-framework): branch/PR/no-self-merge discipline, safe release + rollback tooling, live CT122 quirks, Docker/Compose pitfalls.\"\nversion: 1.1.0\nauthor: Hermes Agent\nlicense: MIT\nplatforms: [linux]\nmetadata:\n  hermes:\n    tags: [ACMS, deployment, rollback, docker-compose, jordan-workflow]\n---\n\n# ACMS Project Operations\n\nACMS (AgentifyMe Cloud Management System) is Jordan's internal AI-workforce\ncontrol plane at `startupteams/acms-project-framework`, live on Proxmox CT122\n(10.0.20.122, MIAM-00135). Local clone: `~/work/acms-project-framework`\n(venv `.venv-acms`, Python 3.12). This skill is the durable operating\nprocedure; the chronological findings log lives in `/home/jordatech/agents.md`\nand current state in memory.\n\n## Authority model (never violate)\n\n- Feature work: branch → PR → **human review only**. Agents do NOT self-merge.\n- Deployment is human-gated, BUT once a release is authorized, rollback to the\n  previous known-good on failed validation is pre-authorized — do not stop and\n  ask mid-rollback.\n- Never hotpatch production code (fix `main` via a corrective PR later).\n- Never run `alembic downgrade` automatically; destructive migrations need\n  explicit human approval.\n\n## Jordan collaboration rules (embedded from corrections)\n\n- Questions before NEW plans, then **full autonomy** — roadblock → pivot,\n  never stop.\n- **All durable notes in ENGLISH** — session notes, handoffs, STEER-LOG,\n  PLAN-OF-RECORD, SESSION-STATE (Jordan's mid-session steer 2026-10-02:\n  \"switch back to english when taking notes, not chinese please\"). Never\n  produce session artifacts in another language.\n- **Never re-gate mid-execution.** If a plan is already approved (his authored\n  plan doc, his \"continue work\"), do not send a clarify/decision prompt; the\n  2026-09-26 session's clarify timed out and he replied \"What are you waiting\n  on me for?\" → proceed, document decisions as you go, report at the end.\n- Deliverable pattern: single `.md` handoff in the work folder + chat summary;\n  every PR/deployment gets a handoff doc listing requirements, validation,\n  backup paths, previous known-good, and next step.\n\n## Interrupted-session handoff format (Jordan-requested 2026-10-01)\n\nWhen a session ends mid-execution or amid confusion, the handoff `.md` must\nhave this shape (executed example:\n`~/acms-a2a-20261001/HANDOFF-20261001-ACMS-A2A-PRODUCTION-PATH.md`):\n\n1. **§0 verified-vs-unverified, READ FIRST** — every directive asserted\n   mid-session WITHOUT a visible user message goes into an explicit\n   \"confirm with Jordan before building on it\" list, with any conflict against\n   the plan doc called out. Tool-verified facts are listed separately from\n   in-window assertions.\n2. **Three explicit state sections:** DONE (each claim tied to its tool\n   output/evidence), STARTED BUT NOT COMPLETED (exact remaining steps per\n   item, enough for the next session to execute), NOT DONE AT ALL (untouched\n   plan sections).\n3. Then: live-state inventory, health-check commands, incidents & security\n   notes, per-service rollback, ordered next-session runbook whose FIRST step\n   is the human confirmations from §0.\n4. Deliver the handoff to Jordan's Telegram (see `messaging/telegram-send-file`).\n\n**Compaction-window discipline (hit live 2026-10-01):** in multi-window\nsessions, assistant prose — INCLUDING self-assessments like \"this session\ndrifted off-plan\" — is NOT ground truth; only tool output is. Before acting on\nor repeating such a claim, `session_search` for the underlying records; if\nthey don't exist, the assertion itself is the confusion artifact (the final\npre-handoff message of 2026-10-01 fabricated record names and falsely declared\nthe whole environment unverified). Conversely, directives that DID arrive but\nwere dropped from the visible transcript are marked unverified, not discarded.\n\n## Usage-pressure safe checkpoint\n\nWhen Jordan warns that session usage is nearly exhausted and asks for a handoff, stop widening scope immediately. Preserve a coherent checkpoint rather than racing an unreviewed deployment: run focused compile/tests/diff checks, commit and push the feature branch, open a **draft PR**, leave production unchanged, and deliver one Markdown handoff with exact verified/unverified status, continuation commands, rollback, and untouched gated phases. Never claim the broader plan complete merely because the checkpoint is durable.\n\n## Jira reconciliation intake — no silent candidate-only success\n\nA `completed` reconciliation run is not proof of intake. Inspect per-issue outcomes: `examined>0` with `added=started=failed=0` and only candidate-observed events means the workflow may have intentionally returned before import. Eligible unlinked issues require an explicit contract: immutable accountId + ready status → fail-closed Project mapping → exactly-once Work/link → idle worker assignment → authoritative `dispatch_service` → bridge ACK. Release an assignment lock on failed/blocked dispatch. Persist a durable outcome for every examined issue and expose it to operators. Completion write-back must reuse one canonical Artifact, post the exact URL, and transition only behind the explicit mutation flag. Full procedure and acceptance proof: `references/jira-reconciliation-intake-and-writeback.md`.\n\n## Release tooling (deploy/ in the repo)\n\n- `deploy/release.sh [<approved-main-sha>]` — full safe transaction: preflight\n  → maintenance window (nginx 503 page + app stop) → verified `pg_dump -Fc`\n  (magic `PGDMP` + size floor + `pg_restore --list` + SHA-256) → SHA-tagged\n  image `acms-app:<short-sha>` → `alembic upgrade head` → two-stage validation\n  (`--stage app` direct under maintenance, `--stage full` via HTTPS proxy) →\n  accept or **auto-rollback** via `rollback.sh --in-flight`.\n- `deploy/rollback.sh` — standalone = Level 1 (app-only, never touches DB,\n  never downgrades); `--in-flight` = Level 2 (checksum-guarded app+DB restore,\n  live DB kept as `acms_old` forensics). DB restore is REFUSED outside an open\n  transaction.\n- `deploy/validate-release.sh --stage app|full --strict-build\n  --expected-rev <rev> [--feature-smoke <cmd>]`.\n- `deploy/smoke-test.sh [base-url]` — standalone operator smoke (not a\n  release gate): backend probes run IN-CONTAINER via `compose exec -T acms-app\n  python` + urllib with exact status asserts (health 200, version 200, unauth\n  agents 401, Work UI 303 via proxy) and exits 1 on any failure. Do NOT\n  reintroduce host-side `127.0.0.1:8000` probes — the app publishes no host\n  ports (nginx is the sole ingress by design). Live-proven 12/12 exit 0 after\n  the 137a008 deploy. **Also: never ship a check-script that counts failures\n  but always exits 0 — smoke-test.sh had exactly that bug (FAILURES counted,\n  no exit 1); scripts that gate anything must propagate failure to the exit\n  code, and `exit 0` printed after a pipeline (`cmd | tail`) is `tail`'s\n  status, not the script's — use `${PIPESTATUS[0]}`.**\n- **Pipeline status (2026-09-26): fully routine.** Drill 3 = \"Release ACCEPTED\"\n  end-to-end (preflight → verified backup → maintenance → build → migrate →\n  ready 2s → 6/6 + 12/12 validation, exit 0); routine deploys #137a008 and\n  #9545123 (v0.5.0) each ACCEPTED with zero intervention, smoke 12/12.\n  **Standing deploy cadence: user message \"PR N merged\" → fetch new main tip →\n  `bash deploy/release.sh <tip-sha>` on CT122 → report ACCEPTED + smoke.** No\n  manual hop unless the on-box tree is somehow behind the transaction baseline\n  (bootstrap-hop rule applies only to first-ever tooling install).\n- **Slice-2 view delivered (9545123, v0.5.0):** Agent Detail page at\n  `/ui/agents/{agent_id}` — read-only; identity + capability manifest\n  (declared vs not-declared flags rendered honestly), assignment/execution-\n  task/handoff history, background-routine inventory (REQ-014) with explicit\n  \"ACMS does not schedule\" note, recorded-only delivery notice (shared\n  `DELIVERY_STATE` constant from work_routes), and an explicit\n  heartbeat-pending note (slice 3) — never fabricate liveness. Reuse\n  `_base_context` + shared constants in new UI routes (a locally re-declared\n  delivery string diverged from the Work UI wording and a version-empty footer\n  appeared; single source of truth fixes both). Home \"not yet implemented\"\n  list must track what the backend actually maintains.\n- **Tooling-self-replacement caveat:** when a target tip CHANGES\n  `deploy/release.sh` (or rollback/validate scripts), the running transaction\n  checks out the new tree mid-flight while bash still reads the old script\n  from disk — unproven territory. The 137a008 deploy was safe only because\n  release.sh itself was byte-identical in the target. After a tooling-changing\n  merge, run a same-tip drill first (re-release the accepted SHA through the\n  new script) before the next real feature deploy — the drill pattern from\n  2026-09-26 (drills 1–3).\n- Release state lives OUTSIDE git at `/opt/acms/releases/`: `current`,\n  `previous`, `history.jsonl`, `releases/<id>.json`, `transaction.json`,\n  `backups/<id>.dump`. Never put secrets in release metadata.\n- Feature-specific smoke hooks via `ACMS_FEATURE_SMOKE` env (plan §11).\n\n## Live CT122 quirks (validated 2026-09-26)\n\n- Git checkout is at `/opt/acms/repo` (NOT `/opt/acms`); `.env` at\n  `/opt/acms/.env` (0600, key names only — never echo values).\n- **Compose v2 container names are `acms-<service>-1`** — bare\n  `docker exec acms-app` FAILS (\"No such container\"). Always\n  `docker compose -f deploy/compose.yaml --env-file /opt/acms/.env exec -T\n  <service>` (service-name agnostic). If a manual hop stage dies on a bare\n  name AFTER copying the maintenance conf, the host\n  `deploy/reverse-proxy/nginx.conf` is already switched —\n  `git checkout -- deploy/reverse-proxy/nginx.conf` BEFORE retrying, or the\n  retry double-applies maintenance content.\n- No `pg_dump`/`psql` on the CT host — run inside the postgres container:\n  `docker compose -f deploy/compose.yaml --env-file /opt/acms/.env exec -T\n  postgres pg_dump -U acms -Fc acms`.\n- No `curl` in the app image — probe with `docker exec acms-acms-app-1\n  python -c \"import urllib.request; ...\"` on 127.0.0.1:8000.\n- nginx allowlists 10.0.10.0/24 + 10.0.20.0/24 — validate via the VM's own IP\n  `https://10.0.20.122` with `curl -k` (self-signed; MARION has NO home.arpa\n  DNS — raw IPs everywhere).\n- `/version` returns `{version, git_sha, git_sha_short, build_time}` — the\n  git_sha identity check catches wrong-image states instantly. Run it after\n  ANY container recreation.\n- LLDAP 10.0.20.101:3890, base dc=example,dc=com; groups acms-admin/16\n  (jordatech), acms-workers/17, acms-observers/18.\n\n## Docker/Compose pitfalls (from the live drill — see references file)\n\n- **Single-file bind mounts pin the inode**: swapping the file on disk is\n  invisible to the container; HUP reload then serves stale config forever.\n  nginx now uses a directory mount (`./reverse-proxy:/etc/nginx/conf.d`) and\n  the maintenance template is named `*.conf.template` so the include glob\n  never loads it. Never re-introduce a single-file conf mount.\n- **`docker compose up -d <svc>` recreates depends_on services too** and can\n  bring the app back with the default image (`acms-app:local`). Pin\n  `ACMS_APP_IMAGE_TAG=<tag>` on EVERY compose up in tooling.\n- **Never command-substitute `compose build`** — its stdout is the build log\n  and pollutes captured variables (`invalid tag`). Send build output to\n  `>&2`; compute the tag, never capture it.\n- **pipefail + `grep -c` false positive**: `grep -c` exits 1 when the count\n  is 0; capture the count into a variable with `|| true`, compare numerically.\n- Recovery from stuck maintenance mode: `up -d --force-recreate\n  reverse-proxy`, then re-pin the app image, then verify via `/version`.\n\n## Bootstrap hop (chicken-and-egg first deploy)\n\nThe first deployment of the release tooling cannot use the tooling. Manual\nhop with identical guarantees: verified backup → stop app → checkout target\nSHA → build SHA-tagged image → migrate → start → validate → write release\nmetadata (mark `mode=manual-bootstrap`). Proven 2026-09-26 (~2 min window).\n\n## Verification hygiene (file-tool corruption lesson)\n\nGround truth for any file change is `git diff` / `py_compile` / `bash -n` —\nnever the tool result echo. Echoes can mislead in BOTH directions (phantom\nerrors and phantom success; this session had mid-write corruption injecting\ngarbage text). For large/critical writes: verify immediately after writing,\nand if corrupted, repair via a python rewrite with assertion pre-checks on\nknown anchor lines, then re-verify with git diff.\n\n## PR-discipline trap (hit TWICE on 2026-09-29 — mandatory habit)\n\n`gh pr merge --squash --delete-branch` checks the LOCAL checkout out onto\n`main` when it deletes the branch. A following `git push -q` (or any commit +\npush) then goes DIRECT to main — Jordan's token has admin rights, so branch\nprotection silently bypasses (remote prints \"Bypassed rule violations\" but the\npush lands). Happened twice in one window (c2d68d7, 8575982; content was\ngreen, the process was violated). **Habit: after every merge-with-delete-branch,\nrun `git branch --show-current` BEFORE any commit; if on main, `git switch -c\n<branch> origin/main` first.** Never `git push` bare from a merge aftermath.\n\n**PERMANENT FIXES now installed on the agent workstation (window-4, plan §D;\nstill keep the habit — the guard is local to this machine):**\n- `~/.config/git/hooks/pre-push` via global `core.hooksPath` — REFUSES\n  pushes to `main|master|production` before the network is touched\n  (behavior-tested in a scratch repo). Emergency override:\n  `ACMS_ALLOW_PROTECTED_PUSH=1 git push …` (audited in shell history).\n  Server-side `gh pr merge` is a GitHub API operation — unaffected.\n- `~/bin/gh-merge <PR#>` — wraps merge-with-delete-branch, then runs the\n  mandatory branch check and auto-detaches to `origin/main` when the merge\n  landed the checkout on main. PATH via `~/.bashrc`.\n- Durable identity fix (least-privilege AI GitHub identity) = staged spike +\n  HUMAN-SETUP file in the flight-work dir; needs Jordan's web-UI action.\n\n## ADR-0012 completion callback (PRs #45/#46, 2026-09-29, live)\n\n- `POST /api/v1/callbacks/execution-completion` — dedicated scoped token\n  `ACMS_CALLBACK_TOKEN` (never the admin token; unset ⇒ 503 fail-closed).\n  CT122 env has CALLBACK_TOKEN (hex) + `ACMS_CALLBACK_BASE_URL=https://10.0.20.122`\n  in /opt/acms/.env; compose passthroughs merged (PR #47, which also added\n  ACMS_JIRA_* + ACMS_GITHUB_ECONOMICS_TOKEN passthroughs).\n- Identity binding: task must belong to the claimed agent (mismatch → 403 +\n  durable EXECUTION_CALLBACK_REJECTED audit; unknown task → 404 + audit).\n  Duplicate/late → idempotent `already_terminal`. Success → no Attention item.\n- **dispatch_service now PRE-creates the execution task before the bridge\n  call**; the task id travels in the instruction (COMPLETION PROTOCOL block,\n  gated on ACMS_CALLBACK_BASE_URL); `external_task_id` = the REAL A2A run id.\n  The dispatch idempotency ledger MOVED to the EXECUTION_DISPATCHED event\n  metadata (dedupe_key → task_id) — code querying `external_task_id ==\n  \"disp-<key>\"` is stale.\n- **Runtime-driven watcher is now the PRIMARY path (window-4, PR #49, prod\n  3679067):** `acms/run_watcher.py` schedules a bounded in-process watcher at\n  dispatch (RunWatchRegistry, idempotent per task_id) that observes the bridge\n  run and delivers the EXACT authenticated callback on terminal state — model\n  compliance NOT required. Retries 0/10/30/60s on transient failure; permanent\n  rejections (403/404/503) stop immediately; watch lifetime bounded (2h\n  default, RUN_WATCH_EXPIRED audit); reconcile remains fallback. Settings:\n  ACMS_CALLBACK_WATCH_ENABLED (default true), ACMS_CALLBACK_SELF_URL (default\n  in-container loopback :8000 — nginx 403s compose-network sources),\n  ACMS_CALLBACK_WATCH_POLL_SECONDS/MAX_SECONDS. **CRITICAL pitfall: the\n  watcher is an asyncio task in the APP process — dispatch MUST go through\n  the API route (`POST /api/v1/dispatch/work/{id}`). Dispatching from a\n  one-shot script process schedules the watcher into a process that exits →\n  nothing watches (hit live: first proof attempt silently fell back to\n  reconcile).**\n- **Live callback-before-reconcile proof (ACMS-WORK-000007, 2026-09-29):**\n  API-route dispatch → worker explicitly instructed NOT to call back →\n  EXECUTION_COMPLETED \"Completion callback: task … → SUCCEEDED\" delivered by\n  the watcher (+50s) with EXECUTION_RECONCILED ABSENT (300s reconcile window\n  not elapsed) → task SUCCEEDED + session CLOSED, zero manual close. Tests:\n  tests/test_run_watcher.py (7). Hermes api-server still has no\n  run-completion webhook — the ACMS-side watcher is what closes that gap.\n- **Window-3 proof pattern (superseded as the completion authority, kept for\n  reconcile-fallback behavior):** dispatch → session auto-open → Hermes run\n  completes (LiteLLM SpendLogs row = cost-of-record) → worker GLM IGNORED the\n  in-instruction completion protocol → the telemetry-scheduler reconcile sweep\n  closed the task from bridge run status + the orphaned OPEN session (both\n  orderings: task-then-session and session-already-orphaned) → zero manual\n  close.\n- Live endpoint probes from the CT HOST work via curl; from INSIDE the app\n  container nginx 403s (container-network source not allowlisted) — same\n  rule as all CT122 API probing.\n\n## Phase B data layer (2026-10-01, prod 11a39de, alembic 0014→0016)\n\n- **Human UIDs:** `ACMS-WORK-######-YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS` / `ACMS-ARTIFACT-######-…`\n  (`acms/uid_keys.py`). # AGENTS.md

Instructions for autonomous and semi-autonomous agents working in ACMS.

## Authority model

Humans set product intent, approve scope, approve significant decisions, define mandatory governance boundaries, and approve merges.

Agents may inspect the repository, plan, implement approved requirements, decompose approved scope into tasks, create tests, update affected documentation, create handoffs, add out-of-scope discoveries to `FUTURE_WORK.md`, draft ADRs/TDRs, and execute non-destructive work inside explicitly approved scope/budget/security/environment boundaries.

Agents must not silently add/remove product scope, promote future work, accept their own new significant ADR, merge their own feature work, exceed a budget, cross tenant/context boundaries, deploy to production, alter credentials/permissions, perform destructive infrastructure actions, or modify protected Organization/Product/Project/Feature context without required human authorization.

## Read before planning

For every meaningful change read:

1. `README.md`
2. `REQUIREMENTS.md`
3. `SPRINT.md`
4. `docs/BUSINESS_PROBLEMS.md`
5. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`
6. relevant accepted/proposed ADRs
7. `FUTURE_WORK.md` when scope/follow-up work is involved
8. `INITIAL_IDEAS.md` when source provenance matters

## Requirement discipline

Implementation must trace to one or more `ACMS-REQ-###` entries. Before editing summarize the requirement IDs, acceptance criteria, files/components likely to change, assumptions, and validation.

If work is outside approved requirements, ask for human approval or record it in `FUTURE_WORK.md`.

## Future-work provenance

Every future-work item must identify one of:

- **Human-Directed**
- **Agent-Discovered**
- **Source-Derived**

Human-Directed does not mean approved; it remains future scope until promoted.

## Centralized governance, decentralized execution

ACMS controls approved assignment, scope, budget, context access, permissions, and governance. It must not become the synchronous permission service for every shell command/tool call/test/retry.

A worker agent may autonomously execute implementation details inside its approved envelope.

An agent may have:

- one primary ACMS work assignment; and
- zero or more registered background routines/cron jobs.

## Context authority

Protected context levels:

- Organization
- Product
- Project
- Feature

Agents may propose changes but may not modify these without human authorization or explicit delegated authority.

Agent, Task, and Live Session context may be modified within approved policy.

If approved scope appears wrong, execute it as far as reasonably possible, preserve state, create a handoff, and propose the alternate direction instead of silently changing scope.

## Handoffs

Meaningful work must produce/update a Markdown handoff recording:

1. assigned goal and requirement IDs;
2. what was attempted;
3. what actually changed;
4. what the agent believes the current state is;
5. validation and results;
6. blockers/assumptions/unresolved questions;
7. recommended next action.

## ADR policy

Human-explicit durable architecture decisions may be recorded as `Accepted`. Agent-originated significant decisions remain `Proposed` until human approval.

## Approval timeout policy

- Reversible in-scope decision: checkpoint/preserve state, use documented fallback, continue if safe.
- Consequential but deferable action: defer that action and continue unrelated work.
- Mandatory human gate: never infer approval from timeout; hold/deny the gated action and continue only unrelated authorized work.

## Security

- Never commit credentials/secrets/customer-sensitive data.
- External/customer agents are a separate trust domain.
- Internal Agent Bridge endpoints are private-network/VPN endpoints unless an accepted ADR says otherwise.
- Do not weaken authentication/authorization/audit to simplify implementation.

## Git workflow

The documentation bootstrap is human-authorized for direct import. After bootstrap, feature work must use a dedicated branch and PR.

Recommended branch: `type/ACMS-short-description`.

Recommended commit: `type(scope): short description (ACMS-REQ-###)` plus issue/ticket when available.

Every feature PR must list requirement IDs, acceptance criteria, validation, risks, ADR/TDR changes, future work, and reviewer focus. Agents do not self-merge.

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Gateway policy engine (plan §16/§17/§19).\n2|\n3|Contract for every capability (tool or resource):\n4|\n5|- ``risk``: READ | SAFE_WRITE | SENSITIVE_WRITE | DESTRUCTIVE\n6|- ``roles``: minimum role set (any-of)\n7|- ``scopes``: minimum scope set (any-of)\n8|- DESTRUCTIVE + caller without executive/infrastructure_admin => hard DENY\n9|- SENSITIVE_WRITE + worker => APPROVAL_REQUIRED (W1: denied-with-reason until\n10|  the approval workflow exists in W2+; policy-gated per plan)\n11|\n12|Tool names use dotted namespaces (``acms.work.update_progress``). Tool-manager\n13|storage keys normalize dots to underscores (SDK constraint); the canonical\n14|dotted name is preserved in ``canonical_name`` and surfaces in tools/list via\n15|the registry layer.\n16|\"\"\"\n17|from __future__ import annotations\n18|\n19|from dataclasses import dataclass, field\n20|from typing import Any, Callable\n21|\n22|from .errors import (\n23|    ApprovalRequiredError,\n24|    DestructiveDenyError,\n25|    RoleRequiredError,\n26|    ScopeRequiredError,\n27|)\n28|from .models import CallIdentity, RiskClass\n29|\n30|SENSITIVE_ROLES = {\"executive\", \"infrastructure_admin\"}\n31|\n32|\n33|@dataclass\n34|class Capability:\n35|    \"\"\"One policy-annotated capability (tool or resource factory).\"\"\"\n36|\n37|    name: str                      # canonical dotted name (tools) or URI template (resources)\n38|    domain: str                    # acms | llm | runtime | proxmox | github | pdu | power | jira | registry | monitoring\n39|    risk: RiskClass\n40|    roles: set[str]\n41|    scopes: set[str]\n42|    description: str = \"\"\n43|    version: int = 1\n44|    timeout_seconds: int = 10\n45|    idempotent: bool = True\n46|    side_effect: str = \"none\"      # none | local | external\n47|    audit: bool = True\n48|    handler: Callable[..., Any] | None = None\n49|    kind: str = \"tool\"             # tool | resource\n50|    uri_template: str | None = None\n51|    output_schema: dict[str, Any] | None = None\n52|    meta: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n53|\n54|    def authorize(self, identity: CallIdentity) -> None:\n55|        \"\"\"Raise typed errors unless the identity may call this capability.\"\"\"\n56|        if self.risk == RiskClass.DESTRUCTIVE:\n57|            # W1 hard rule: DESTRUCTIVE is denied for EVERYONE through the\n58|            # gateway (executive path exists only via an audited emergency\n59|            # policy — not implemented yet). Plan §16/§43.\n60|            raise DestructiveDenyError(\n61|                f\"{self.name} is DESTRUCTIVE; denied at the gateway (W1 policy)\"\n62|            )\n63|        if self.risk == RiskClass.SENSITIVE_WRITE and not identity.has_role(*SENSITIVE_ROLES):\n64|            # W1: no approval workflow yet — fail closed with an actionable reason.\n65|            raise ApprovalRequiredError(\n66|                f\"{self.name} is SENSITIVE_WRITE and requires approval/role gate \"\n67|                f\"(caller roles: {','.join(identity.roles) or 'none'})\"\n68|            )\n69|        if self.risk == RiskClass.SENSITIVE_WRITE and identity.has_role(*SENSITIVE_ROLES):\n70|            return  # executives/admins ARE the approval authority (plan §16)\n71|        if self.roles and not identity.has_role(*self.roles):\n72|            raise RoleRequiredError(\n73|                f\"{self.name} requires one of {sorted(self.roles)}; \"\n74|                f\"caller has {sorted(identity.roles)}\"\n75|            )\n76|        if self.scopes and not any(s in identity.scopes for s in self.scopes):\n77|            raise ScopeRequiredError(\n78|                f\"{self.name} requires one of {sorted(self.scopes)}; \"\n79|                f\"caller has {sorted(identity.scopes)}\"\n80|            )\n81|\n82|\n83|def dotted(name: str) -> str:\n84|    \"\"\"Storage-key normalization: dots -> underscores (SDK schema constraint).\"\"\"\n85|    return name.replace(\".\", \"_\")\n86|\n87|\n88|def canonical(dotted_name: str) -> str:\n89|    \"\"\"Best-effort dotted canonical name from a normalized storage key.\"\"\"\n90|    return dotted_name.replace(\"_\", \".\", 1) if \"_\" in dotted_name and \".\" not in dotted_name else dotted_name\n91|\n92|\n93|class PolicyRegistry:\n94|    \"\"\"Central capability registry (plan §37) with authorization checks.\"\"\"\n95|\n96|    def __init__(self) -> None:\n97|        self._capabilities: dict[str, Capability] = {}\n98|\n99|    def register(self, cap: Capability) -> Capability:\n100|        if cap.kind == \"tool\":\n101|            key = dotted(cap.name)\n102|        else:\n103|            key = cap.name  # resources keyed by URI template\n104|        if key in self._capabilities:\n105|            raise ValueError(f\"duplicate capability key: {key}\")\n106|        self._capabilities[key] = cap\n107|        return cap\n108|\n109|    def tool_for_storage_key(self, storage_key: str) -> Capability | None:\n110|        return self._capabilities.get(storage_key)\n111|\n112|    def resource_for_uri(self, uri: str) -> tuple[Capability, dict[str, str]] | None:\n113|        \"\"\"Match a concrete resource URI against registered URI templates.\"\"\"\n114|        for cap in self._capabilities.values():\n115|            if cap.kind != \"resource\" or not cap.uri_template:\n116|                continue\n117|            params = match_uri_template(cap.uri_template, uri)\n118|            if params is not None:\n119|                return cap, params\n120|        return None\n121|\n122|    def list_capabilities(self) -> list[Capability]:\n123|        return sorted(self._capabilities.values(), key=lambda c: (c.domain, c.kind, c.name))\n124|\n125|    def __len__(self) -> int:\n126|        return len(self._capabilities)\n127|\n128|\n129|import re  # Returns params\n136|    or None. Segment counts must match; params never span '/'; url-decoding\n137|    is the caller's job (URIs here are already percent-decoded by MCP).\"\"\"\n138|    tparts = template.strip(\"/\").split(\"/\")\n139|    uparts = uri.strip(\"/\").split(\"/\")\n140|    if len(tparts) != len(uparts):\n141|        return None\n142|    params: dict[str, str] = {}\n143|    for tp, up in zip(tparts, uparts):\n144|        m = _TEMPLATE_PARAM.fullmatch(tp)\n145|        if m:\n146|            params[m.group(1)] = up\n147|        elif tp != up:\n148|            return None\n149|    return params\n150|", "total_lines": 149, "file_size": 5927, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Gateway data models (plan §12/§13/§14/§16/§17/§19/§24).\"\"\"\n2|from __future__ import annotations\n3|\n4|from datetime import datetime, timezone\n5|from enum import StrEnum\n6|import hashlib\n7|import json\n8|import uuid\n9|\n10|from typing import Any\n11|\n12|from pydantic import BaseModel, Field\n13|\n14|\n15|def utcnow() -> datetime:\n16|    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n17|\n18|\n19|class RiskClass(StrEnum):\n20|    READ = \"READ\"\n21|    SAFE_WRITE = \"SAFE_WRITE\"\n22|    SENSITIVE_WRITE = \"SENSITIVE_WRITE\"\n23|    DESTRUCTIVE = \"DESTRUCTIVE\"\n24|\n25|\n26|class Role(StrEnum):\n27|    WORKER = \"worker\"\n28|    REVIEWER = \"reviewer\"\n29|    PROJECT_MANAGER = \"project_manager\"\n30|    EXECUTIVE = \"executive\"\n31|    INFRASTRUCTURE_ADMIN = \"infrastructure_admin\"\n32|    OBSERVER = \"observer\"\n33|\n34|\n35|class TokenKind(StrEnum):\n36|    AGENT = \"agent\"\n37|    ASSIGNMENT = \"assignment\"\n38|\n39|\n40|class AgentTokenRecord(BaseModel):\n41|    \"\"\"Agent-scoped identity token (plan §12): revocable, narrow, expiring.\"\"\"\n42|\n43|    token_id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))\n44|    token_hash: str  # sha256 hex of the raw token; raw NEVER stored\n45|    agent_name: str\n46|    acms_agent_id: str | None = None\n47|    roles: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)\n48|    scopes: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)  # e.g. [\"acms.read\", \"acms.write\"]\n49|    created_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=utcnow)\n50|    expires_at: datetime | None = None\n51|    revoked_at: datetime | None = None\n52|    revoked_reason: str | None = None\n53|    # Optional bind: token accepted only from these source IP prefixes (defense-in-depth)\n54|    source_ip_allowlist: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)\n55|\n56|    def is_active(self, now: datetime | None = None) -> bool:\n57|        now = now or utcnow()\n58|        if self.revoked_at is not None:\n59|            return False\n60|        if self.expires_at is not None and self.expires_at <= now:\n61|            return False\n62|        return True\n63|\n64|\n65|class AssignmentTokenRecord(BaseModel):\n66|    \"\"\"Assignment-scoped token (plan §13): short-lived, bound to Work/Project/Jira.\"\"\"\n67|\n68|    token_id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))\n69|    token_hash: str\n70|    agent_name: str\n71|    work_uid: str\n72|    project_id: str | None = None\n73|    project_slug: str | None = None\n74|    jira_issue_key: str | None = None\n75|    repositories: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)\n76|    tools: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)\n77|    scopes: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)\n78|    created_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=utcnow)\n79|    expires_at: datetime | None = None\n80|    revoked_at: datetime | None = None\n81|    revoked_reason: str | None = None\n82|\n83|    def is_active(self, now: datetime | None = None) -> bool:\n84|        now = now or utcnow()\n85|        if self.revoked_at is not None:\n86|            return False\n87|        if self.expires_at is not None and self.expires_at <= now:\n88|            return False\n89|        return True\n90|\n91|\n92|class CallIdentity(BaseModel):\n93|    \"\"\"Resolved identity for one MCP call (set by middleware, read by handlers).\"\"\"\n94|\n95|    token_kind: TokenKind\n96|    token_id: str\n97|    agent_name: str\n98|    acms_agent_id: str | None = None\n99|    roles: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)\n100|    scopes: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)\n101|    # assignment-scoped extras (None for agent tokens)\n102|    work_uid: str | None = None\n103|    project_id: str | None = None\n104|    project_slug: str | None = None\n105|    jira_issue_key: str | None = None\n106|    repositories: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)\n107|\n108|    @property\n109|    def is_assignment_scoped(self) -> bool:\n110|        return self.token_kind == TokenKind.ASSIGNMENT\n111|\n112|    def has_role(self, *roles: str) -> bool:\n113|        return any(r in self.roles for r in roles)\n114|\n115|\n116|class AuditEntry(BaseModel):\n117|    \"\"\"MCP activity log row (plan §24) — no secrets, arguments HASHED only.\"\"\"\n118|\n119|    ts: datetime = Field(default_factory=utcnow)\n120|    agent_name: str\n121|    agent_id: str | None = None\n122|    token_id: str\n123|    token_kind: str\n124|    work_uid: str | None = None\n125|    domain: str\n126|    name: str  # resource/tool full name\n127|    entry_kind: str  # resource | tool | capabilities | health\n128|    risk_class: RiskClass = RiskClass.READ\n129|    args_hash: str | None = None\n130|    status: str  # ok | denied | error\n131|    http_status: int | None = None\n132|    duration_ms: int | None = None\n133|    approval: str | None = None\n134|    error_type: str | None = None\n135|    detail: str | None = None\n136|    request_id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))\n137|\n138|\n139|def hash_args(args: dict[str, Any] | None) -> str | None:\n140|    \"\"\"SHA-256 of canonical JSON args (plan §24: never store raw arguments).\"\"\"\n141|    if not args:\n142|        return None\n143|    canonical = json.dumps(args, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), default=str)\n144|    return hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()\n145|\n146|\n147|def new_raw_token(prefix: str) -> str:\n148|    \"\"\"Generate a raw bearer token. Raw shown ONCE to the operator; only the\n149|    sha256 hash is stored (plan §12: never print tokens in logs/handoffs).\"\"\"\n150|    return f\"{prefix}_{uuid.uuid4().hex}{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}\"\n151|", "total_lines": 150, "file_size": 4932, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

rec.roles,\n81|        \"scopes\": rec.scopes,\n82|        \"expires_at\": rec.expires_at.isoformat() if rec.expires_at else None,\n83|        # raw appears here ONCE — capture immediately, store 0600 on the agent\n84|        \"token\": raw,\n85|    }, indent=2))\n86|    print(f\"# NOTE: raw token shown ONCE. Store at 0600 on the agent; never log it.\",\n87|          file=sys.stderr)\n88|    return 0\n89|\n90|\n91|def cmd_mint_assignment(args) -> int:\n92|    cfg = _config_from_env()\n93|    store = kwargs[\"jira_issue_key\"] = args.jira\n99|    if args.repo:\n100|        kwargs[\"repositories\"] = list(args.repo)\n101|    rec, raw = store.mint_assignment_token(\n102| \"expires_at\": rec.expires_at.isoformat() if rec.expires_at else None,\n112|        \"token\": raw,\n113|    }, indent=2))\n114|    print(\"# NOTE: raw token shown ONCE.\", file=sys.stderr)\n115| rows.append({\n123|            \"kind\": \"agent\", \"token_id\": t.token_id, \"agent_name\": t.agent_name,\n124|            \"acms_agent_id\": t.acms_agent_id, \"roles\": t.roles, \"scopes\": ### [2026-10-02T08:02:50.528596+00:00] tool · tool: read_file

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Durable gateway token store (plan §12/§13).\n2|\n3|Design: the gateway keeps its OWN SQLite token store (``tokens.sqlite3``) so\n4|that gateway identity is fully independent of ACMS schema/migrations (the\n5|gateway stays additive; ACMS keeps zero MCP tables in W1). Store is\n6|process-safe for the single gateway service process; writes are transactional.\n7|\n8|Raw tokens are NEVER persisted — only sha256 hashes. ``mint_agent_token`` /\n9|``mint_assignment_token`` return the raw token exactly once to the operator.\n10|\"\"\"\n11|from __future__ import annotations\n12|\n13|import hashlib\n14|import json\n15|import os\n16|import sqlite3\n17|import threading\n18|from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n19|from pathlib import Path\n20|from typing import Any\n21|\n22|from .models import (\n23|    AgentTokenRecord,\n24|    AssignmentTokenRecord,\n25|    new_raw_token,\n26|    utcnow,\n27|)\n28|\n29|_SCHEMA = \"\"\"\n30|CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_tokens (\n31|    token_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n32|    token_hash TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,\n33|    agent_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n34|    acms_agent_id TEXT,\n35|    roles_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',\n36|    scopes_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',\n37|    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n38|    expires_at TEXT,\n39|    revoked_at TEXT,\n40|    revoked_reason TEXT,\n41|    source_ip_allowlist_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'\n42|);\n43|CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS assignment_tokens (\n44|    token_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n45|    token_hash TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,\n46|    agent_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n47|    work_uid TEXT NOT NULL,\n48|    project_id TEXT,\n49|    project_slug TEXT,\n50|    jira_issue_key TEXT,\n51|    repositories_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',\n52|    tools_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',\n53|    scopes_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',\n54|    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n55|    expires_at TEXT,\n56|    revoked_at TEXT,\n57|    revoked_reason TEXT\n58|);\n59|CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_agent_tokens_hash ON agent_tokens(token_hash);\n60|CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_assignment_tokens_hash ON assignment_tokens(token_hash);\n61|CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_assignment_tokens_agent ON assignment_tokens(agent_name, work_uid);\n62|\"\"\"\n63|\n64|\n65|def _hash(raw: str) -> str:\n66|    return hashlib.sha256(raw.encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()\n67|\n68|\n69|def _iso(dt: datetime | None) -> str | None:\n70|    if dt is None:\n71|        return None\n72|    if dt.tzinfo is None:\n73|        dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n74|    return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n75|\n76|\n77|def _parse(value: str | None) -> list[str]:\n78|    if not value:\n79|        return []\n80|    try:\n81|        parsed = json.loads(value)\n82|        return list(parsed) if isinstance(parsed, list) else []\n83|    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n84|        return []\n85|\n86|\n87|def _row_to_agent(row: sqlite3.Row) -> AgentTokenRecord:\n88|    return AgentTokenRecord(\n89|        token_id=row[\"token_id\"],\n90|        token_hash=row[\"token_hash\"],\n91|        agent_name=row[\"agent_name\"],\n92|        acms_agent_id=row[\"acms_agent_id\"],\n93|        roles=_parse(row[\"roles_json\"]),\n94|        scopes=_parse(row[\"scopes_json\"]),\n95|        created_at=datetime.fromisoformat(row[\"created_at\"]),\n96|        expires_at=datetime.fromisoformat(row[\"expires_at\"]) if row[\"expires_at\"] else None,\n97|        revoked_at=datetime.fromisoformat(row[\"revoked_at\"]) if row[\"revoked_at\"] else None,\n98|        revoked_reason=row[\"revoked_reason\"],\n99|        source_ip_allowlist=_parse(row[\"source_ip_allowlist_json\"]),\n100|    )\n101|\n102|\n103|def _row_to_assignment(row: sqlite3.Row) -> AssignmentTokenRecord:\n104|    return AssignmentTokenRecord(\n105|        token_id=row[\"token_id\"],\n106|        token_hash=row[\"token_hash\"],\n107|        agent_name=row[\"agent_name\"],\n108|        work_uid=row[\"work_uid\"],\n109|        project_id=row[\"project_id\"],\n110|        project_slug=row[\"project_slug\"],\n111|        jira_issue_key=row[\"jira_issue_key\"],\n112|        repositories=_parse(row[\"repositories_json\"]),\n113|        tools=_parse(row[\"tools_json\"]),\n114|        scopes=_parse(row[\"scopes_json\"]),\n115|        created_at=datetime.fromisoformat(row[\"created_at\"]),\n116|        expires_at=datetime.fromisoformat(row[\"expires_at\"]) if row[\"expires_at\"] else None,\n117|        revoked_at=datetime.fromisoformat(row[\"revoked_at\"]) if row[\"revoked_at\"] else None,\n118|        revoked_reason=row[\"revoked_reason\"],\n119|    )\n120|\n121|", "total_lines": 290, "file_size": {"content": "121|\n122|class TokenStore:\n123|    def __init__(self, path: str | Path):\n124|        path = Path(path)\n125|        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n126|        if path.exists():\n127|            #defense: never inherit a non-database file\n128|            probe = path.read_bytes()[:16] if path.stat().st_size >= 16 else b\"\"\n129|            if probe and not probe.startswith(b\"SQLite format 3\"):\n130|                raise ValueError(f\"token store path {path} exists and is not a SQLite database\")\n131|        self._conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False)\n132|        self._conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n133|        self._lock = threading.Lock()\n134|        with self._lock:\n135|            self._conn.executescript(_SCHEMA)\n136|            self._conn.commit()\n137|\n138|    # ---------------- mint (raw returned ONCE) ----------------\n139|\n140|    def mint_agent_token(\n141|        self, agent_name: str, *, acms_agent_id: str | None = None,\n142|        roles: list[str] | None = None, scopes: list[str] | None = None,\n143|        ttl_days: int | None = 365, source_ip_allowlist: list[str] | None = None,\n144|    ) -> tuple[AgentTokenRecord, str]:\n145|        raw = new_raw_token(\"mcp\")\n146|        now = utcnow()\n147|        rec = AgentTokenRecord(\n148|            token_hash=_hash(raw),\n149|            agent_name=agent_name,\n150|            acms_agent_id=acms_agent_id,\n151|            roles=roles or [\"worker\"],\n152|            scopes=scopes or [\"acms.read\", \"acms.write\"],\n153|            created_at=now,\n154|            expires_at=now + timedelta(days=ttl_days) if ttl_days is not None else None,\n155|            source_ip_allowlist=source_ip_allowlist or [],\n156|        )\n157|        with self._lock, self._conn:\n158|            self._conn.execute(\n159|                \"INSERT INTO agent_tokens (token_id, token_hash, agent_name, acms_agent_id, \"\n160|                \"roles_json, scopes_json, created_at, expires_at, source_ip_allowlist_json) \"\n161|                \"VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)\",\n162|                (rec.token_id, rec.token_hash, rec.agent_name, rec.acms_agent_id,\n163|                 json.dumps(rec.roles), json.dumps(rec.scopes), _iso(rec.created_at),\n164|                 _iso(rec.expires_at), json.dumps(rec.source_ip_allowlist)),\n165|            )\n166|        return rec, raw\n167|\n168|    def mint_assignment_token(\n169|        self, *, agent_name: str, work_uid: str, agent_token_raw: str | None = None,\n170|        agent_token_hash: str | None = None, ttl_hours: int = 24,\n171|        project_id: str | None = None, project_slug: str | None = None,\n172|        jira_issue_key: str | None = None, repositories: list[str] | None = None,\n173|        tools: list[str] | None = None, scopes: list[str] | None = None,\n174|    ) -> tuple[AssignmentTokenRecord, str]:\n175|        \"\"\"Mint an assignment token. Authenticated by EITHER the agent's raw token\n176|        (verified here — never stored) or its hash (operator path).\"\"\"\n177|        if agent_token_raw:\n178|            agent_rec = self.resolve_agent_token(agent_token_raw)\n179|            if agent_rec is None:\n180|                raise PermissionError(\"agent token not recognized\")\n181|            if agent_rec.agent_name != agent_name:\n182|                raise PermissionError(\"agent token does not belong to the named agent\")\n183|        elif agent_token_hash:\n184|            with self._lock:\n185|                row = self._conn.execute(\n186|                    \"SELECT * FROM agent_tokens WHERE token_hash=?\", (agent_token_hash,)\n187|                ).fetchone()\n188|            if row is None:\n189|                raise PermissionError(\"agent token not recognized\")\n190|            agent_rec = _row_to_agent(row)\n191|            if agent_rec.agent_name != agent_name:\n192|                raise PermissionError(\"agent token does not belong to the named agent\")\n193|        else:\n194|            raise PermissionError(\"mint_assignment_token requires the agent's raw token or its hash\")\n195|\n196|        raw = new_raw_token(\"mcpt\")\n197|        now = utcnow()\n198|        rec = AssignmentTokenRecord(\n199|            token_hash=_hash(raw),\n200|            agent_name=agent_name,\n201|            work_uid=work_uid,\n202|            project_id=project_id,\n203|            project_slug=project_slug,\n204|            jira_issue_key=jira_issue_key,\n205|            repositories=repositories or [],\n206|            tools=tools or [],\n207|            scopes=scopes or [\"acms.read\", \"acms.write\"],\n208|            created_at=now,\n209|            expires_at=now + timedelta(hours=ttl_hours),\n210|        )\n211|        with self._lock, self._conn:\n212|            self._conn.execute(\n213|                \"INSERT INTO assignment_tokens (token_id, token_hash, agent_name, work_uid, \"\n214|                \"project_id, project_slug, jira_issue_key, repositories_json, tools_json, \"\n215|                \"scopes_json, created_at, expires_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)\",\n216|                (rec.token_id, rec.token_hash, rec.agent_name, rec.work_uid, rec.project_id,\n217|                 rec.project_slug, rec.jira_issue_key, json.dumps(rec.repositories),\n218|                 json.dumps(rec.tools), json.dumps(rec.scopes), _iso(rec.created_at),\n219|                 _iso(rec.expires_at)),\n220|            )\n221|        return rec, raw\n222|\n223|    # ---------------- resolution / lifecycle ----------------\n224|\n225|    def resolve_agent_token(self, raw: str) -> AgentTokenRecord | None:\n226|        with self._lock:\n227|            row = self._conn.execute(\n228|                \"SELECT * FROM agent_tokens WHERE token_hash=?\", (_hash(raw),)\n229|            ).fetchone()\n230|        return _row_to_agent(row) if row else None\n231|\n232|    def resolve_assignment_token(self, raw: str) -> AssignmentTokenRecord | None:\n233|        with self._lock:\n234|            row = self._conn.execute(\n235|                \"SELECT * FROM assignment_tokens WHERE token_hash=?\", (_hash(raw),)\n236|            ).fetchone()\n237|        return _row_to_assignment(row) if row else None\n238|\n239|    def list_agent_tokens(self, *, include_revoked: bool = True) -> list[AgentTokenRecord]:\n240|        with self._lock:\n241|            rows = self._conn.execute(\n242|                \"SELECT * FROM agent_tokens ORDER BY created_at DESC\"\n243|            ).fetchall()\n244|        out = [_row_to_agent(r) for r in rows]\n245|        if not include_revoked:\n246|            out = [t for t in out if t.is_active()]\n247|        return out\n248|\n249|    def list_assignment_tokens(self, *, agent_name: str | None = None,\n250|                               work_uid: str | None = None,\n251|                               include_revoked: bool = True) -> list[AssignmentTokenRecord]:\n252|        q = \"SELECT * FROM assignment_tokens\"\n253|        clauses, params = [], []\n254|        if agent_name:\n255|            clauses.append(\"agent_name=?\")\n256|            params.append(agent_name)\n257|        if work_uid:\n258|            clauses.append(\"work_uid=?\")\n259|            params.append(work_uid)\n260|        if clauses:\n261|            q += \" WHERE \" + \" AND \".join(clauses)\n262|        q += \" ORDER BY created_at DESC\"\n263|        with self._lock:\n264|            rows = self._conn.execute(q, params).fetchall()\n265|        out = [_row_to_assignment(r) for r in rows]\n266|        if not include_revoked:\n267|            out = [t for t in out if t.is_active()]\n268|        return out\n269|\n270|    def revoke_agent_token(self, token_id: str, reason: str) -> bool:\n271|        with self._lock, self._conn:\n272|            cur = self._conn.execute(\n273|                \"UPDATE agent_tokens SET revoked_at=?, revoked_reason=? \"\n274|                \"WHERE token_id=? AND revoked_at IS NULL\",\n275|                (_iso(utcnow()), reason, token_id),\n276|            )\n277|        return cur.rowcount > 0\n278|\n279|    def revoke_assignment_token(self, token_id: str, reason: str) -> bool:\n280|        with self._lock, self._conn:\n281|            cur = self._conn.execute(\n282|                \"UPDATE assignment_tokens SET revoked_at=?, revoked_reason=? \"\n283|                \"WHERE token_id=? AND revoked_at IS NULL\",\n284|                (_iso(utcnow()), reason, token_id),\n285|            )\n286|        return cur.rowcount > 0\n287|\n288|    def close(self) -> None:\n289|        with self._lock:\n290|            self._conn.close()\n291|", "total_lines": 290, "file_size": 11750, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

### [2026-10-02T08:03:06.544190+00:00] tool · tool: read_file

{"content": "1|\"\"\"ACMS adapter (plan §18): the FIRST domain adapter.\n2|\n3|The gateway calls ACMS REST (the domain authority) over HTTPS with the\n4|gateway's own bearer credential (ACMS_ADMIN_TOKEN scope; later a dedicated\n5|service token). No DB session is opened here in production paths — REST is\n6|the boundary, keeping the gateway independently fail-able (plan §33: gateway\n7|failure never breaks ACMS, and ACMS failure never breaks REST/A2A/SSE).\n8|\n9|Surface (plan §18):\n10|\n11|Resources (READ):\n12|  acms.work.get            (URI: acms://work/{work_uid_or_id})\n13|  acms.work.current        (URI: acms://assignment/current)\n14|  acms.project.get         (URI: acms://project/{project_id_or_slug})\n15|  acms.product.get         (URI: acms://product/{product_id})\n16|  acms.artifact.get        (URI: acms://artifact/{artifact_uid_or_id})\n17|  acms.agent.self          (URI: acms://agent/self)\n18|  acms.policy.current      (URI: acms://policy/current)\n19|  acms.inbox.current       (URI: acms://inbox/current)\n20|  acms.context.current     (URI: acms://context/current)  <- plan §14 manifest\n21|\n22|Worker tools (SAFE_WRITE unless noted):\n23|  acms.work.update_progress     (scope: own assignment)\n24|  acms.work.mark_blocked        (scope: own assignment)\n25|  acms.artifact.create          (scope: own assignment)\n26|  acms.work.submit_handoff      (scope: own assignment)\n27|  acms.inbox.raise              (scope: own assignment; severity-bounded)\n28|  acms.review.request           (scope: own assignment)\n29|\n30|Scope rules:\n31|- agent token (worker role): may read Work/Project/Product/Artifacts linked to\n32|  its CURRENT ACTIVE assignment + general non-sensitive metadata; may write\n33|  only within its own assignment (work/assignment/artifact linkage enforced).\n34|- assignment token: narrowed to exactly its Work UID (+ project/jira binding).\n35|- executive/infrastructure_admin: broader reads; default verification\n75|\n76|    def request(self, method: str, path: str, payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n77|                query: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> tuple[int, Any]:\n78|        url = f\"{self.base_url}{path}\"\n79|        if query:\n80|            from urllib.parse import urlencode\n81|            url += \"?\" + urlencode(query)\n82|        data = None\n83|        headers = {\"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {self._token}\", \"Accept\": \"application/json\"}\n84|        if payload is not None:\n85|            data = json.dumps(payload).encode(\"utf-8\")\n86|            headers[\"Content-Type\"] = \"application/json\"\n87|        req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, headers=headers, method=method)\n88|        try:\n89|            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout, context=self._ctx) as resp:\n90|                body = resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n91|                status = resp.status\n92|        except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:\n93|            body = exc.read().decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n94|            status = exc.code\n95|        except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as exc:\n96|            raise DomainUnavailableError(f\"ACMS unreachable: {exc.__class__.__name__}\") from exc\n97|        try:\n98|            parsed = json.loads(body) if body else None\n99|        except ValueError:\n100|            parsed = body\n101|        return status, parsed\n102|\n103|    # None when absent.\"\"\"\n107|        if not ref:\n108|            return None\n109|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", f\"/api/v1/work/items/{ref}\")\n110|        if status == 200:\n111|            return body\n112|        # list + filter by UID/key (the detail route may accept only UUIDs)\n113|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", \"/api/v1/work/items\")\n114|        if status != 200 or not isinstance(body, list):\n115|            raise DomainUnavailableError(f\"ACMS work list failed (HTTP {status})\")\n116|        for item in body:\n117|            if ref in (item.get(\"work_item_id\"), item.get(\"work_key\"), item.get(\"work_uid\")):\n118|                return item\n119|        return None\n120|\n121|    def active_assignment(self, agent_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n122|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", \"/api/v1/work/assignments\", query={\"agent_id\": agent_id, \"status\": \"ACTIVE\"})\n123|        if status != 200 or not isinstance(body, list):\n124|            raise DomainUnavailableError(f\"ACMS assignments failed (HTTP {status})\")\n125|        return body[0] if body else None\n126|\n127|    def get_project(self, ref: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n128|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", f\"/projects/{ref}\")\n129|        if status == 200:\n130|            return body\n131|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", \"/projects\")\n132|        if status != 200 or not isinstance(body, list):\n133|            raise DomainUnavailableError(f\"ACMS projects failed (HTTP {status})\")\n134|        for p in body:\n135|            if ref in (p.get(\"project_id\"), p.get(\"slug\"), p.get(\"name\")):\n136|                return p\n137|        return None\n138|\n139|    def get_product(self, product_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n140|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", f\"/products/{product_id}\")\n141|        if status == 200:\n142|            return body\n143|        return None\n144|\n145|    def find_artifact(self, ref: str, *, work_item_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n146|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", f\"/artifacts/{ref}\")\n147|        if status == 200:\n148|            return body\n149|        # artifact detail route accepts UUID/UID/sha; fall back to list filter\n150|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", \"/artifacts\")\n151|        if status != 200 or not isinstance(body, list):\n152|            raise DomainUnavailableError(f\"ACMS artifacts failed (HTTP {status})\")\n153|        for a in body:\n154|            if ref in (a.get(\"artifact_id\"), a.get(\"artifact_uid\")):\n155|                return a\n156|        return None\n157|\n158|    def artifact_content(self, artifact_id: str) -> str | None:\n159|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", f\"/artifacts/{artifact_id}/content\")\n160|        if status != 200:\n161|            return None\n162|        if isinstance(body, dict):\n163|            return body.get(\"content\")\n164|        return body if isinstance(body, str) else None\n165|\n166|    def get_agent(self, agent_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n167|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", f\"/api/v1/agents/{agent_id}\")\n168|        if status == 200:\n169|            return body\n170|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", \"/api/v1/agents\")\n171|        if status != 200 or not isinstance(body, list):\n172|            raise DomainUnavailableError(f\"ACMS agents failed (HTTP {status})\")\n173|        for a in body:\n174|            if ref_match(agent_id, a):\n175|                return a\n176|        return None\n177|\n178|    # Local: `bash deploy/build-release.sh . dist` → tar.gz + sha256.\n   Build the artifact from the MAIN TIP, not your feature branch: create a\n   throwaway `git switch -c tmp-deploy-<sha> origin/main`, build from there,\n   then switch back (2026-09-29 pattern; keeps the artifact == merged main).\n2. Deploy-flow exceptions where qga/node-SSH is still required:\n  see `vm114-qga-ssh-recovery` skill.\n\n## ARM reconciler semantics (services/reconciliation.py)\n\n- desired RUNNING: observe → start owned VM once (ownership-verified, bounded\n  retries w/ capped backoff, `recovery_count`) → RUNNING; PVE truth corrects\n  stale DB actuals.\n- desired STOPPED: graceful shutdown once → **bounded convergence wait**\n  (`ARM_RECONCILE_SHUTDOWN_WAIT_S`, default 30s/3s polls). PVE\n  unreachable → record `last_error`, no state flip on unknown ground truth.\n- Single-replica discipline (TDR-0009): one uvicorn worker; no leader election\n  yet. state-sync health vocabulary: PENDING|SYNCING|VERIFIED|STALE|FAILED.\n- Stale failed-provisioning rows: disposition is **SUPERSEDED** (never delete).\n  Admin endpoint marks each failed attempt row SUPERSEDED and links it to the\n  live runtime (5 ERROR rows → live VM124 runtime 56c1b849, 2026-09-28, PR\n  #50). Backoff 10/20/40/60s capped\n  (attempts vary). Every attempt audits `failure_class` /\n  `recovery_method` / `attempt` in the RuntimeEvent detail (durable audit log\n  IS the recovery-method history — no second mutable table). Do not edit prod files in place.\n\n## Server Manager API (:8300) — auth pattern\n\n- Scoped bearer identities from `/etc/llm-manager/secrets/service_tokens`\n  (0600, outside Git). **File format trap:** lines are `service=token:scopes`.\n  `cut -d= -f2` yields `token:scopes` — you MUST split off everything from the\n  first colon or every call 401s. Token staged locally at\n  `~/.sm_service_token_acms` (0600, never echo).\n- Scopes: read = runtime:read, job:read, route:read, usage:read, health:read;\n  write = runtime:write, runtime:destroy, job:submit. 401 first, then 403.\n- Endpoints (v1.0.0 + 2026-09-28 additions): `/api/v1/agent-runtimes[/{id}]`,\n  `/desired-state`, `/agent-runtimes/{id}/reconcile`, `/reconcile`,\n  `/agent-runtimes/{id}/state-sync`, `/api/v1/model-routes[/{route}]`,\n  `/api/v1/hosts`, `/api/v1/usage`, `/api/v1/pdu/*` (health, capabilities,\n  assets, asset power-state, action-plans=DRY-RUN, jobs, audit; actuation\n  env-gated `SERVER_MANAGER_PDU_ACTUATION=1`, `off` refused at SM layer).\n  New 2026-10-01 (PR #74, prod fce5ba7): **`GET /api/v1/facility/power`**\n  (scope `usage:read`) — per-channel (PDU MIAM-00151/152/153 + mini split) +\n  TOTAL MARION_IA_USA energy/cost for ACMS ingest; stale → NULL + STALE +\n  timestamp (never 0), total withheld with `incomplete_reason` when any\n  channel stale; rate from `electricity_rates` components else plan v0.2.1.\n  Self-contained SQL over `get_session_factory(\"llm\")` — deliberately does\n  NOT import the web app's `power_view` (layout boundary). ACMS's\n  `FacilityPowerClient` consumes this with the SAME svc-acms token.\n\n## VM114 release transaction (proven loop)\n\n1. Local: `bash deploy/build-release.sh . dist` → tar.gz + sha256.\n   Build the artifact from the MAIN TIP, not your feature branch: create a\n   throwaway `git switch -c tmp-deploy-<sha> origin/main`, build from there,\n   then switch back (2026-09-29 pattern; keeps the artifact == merged main).\n2. Serve: `python3 -m http.server 8899 --bind 0.0.0.0` from `dist/`\n   (Hermes background terminal); kill it after (`pkill -f 'http.server 8899'`).\n   **Simpler than http.server: `scp` the tarball+sha directly to\n   `vm114:/tmp/`** (VM114 has sshd via the dedicated key) — used successfully\n   2026-09-29 for both releases.\n3. On VM114: `sha256sum -c <tarball>.sha256` → OK. **The `.sha256` file must\n   be named `<tarball>.sha256` and sit next to it** — the build names the sha\n   after the CONTENT hash; rewrite the single line first\n   (`python -c` writing `<content-hash>  <tarball-name>`) or preflight aborts.\n   Also: **build-release.sh names tarballs with the SHORT (12-char) sha**\n   (`llm-manager-8b05afb5c586.tar.gz`) while GitHub artifact uploads use the\n   full `github.sha` — never match an artifact by full sha.\n4. Run the CURRENT deployment's `deploy-release.sh` (`/opt/llm-manager/current/deploy/deploy-release.sh <tarball>`)\n   via `setsid nohup ... > /tmp/deploy-<sha>.log 2>&1 < /dev/null &` (plain\n   nohup from a ssh 'bash -c' dies with the session; setsid survives).\n   **EXCEPTION — tooling-changing merges:** when the artifact CHANGES\n   deploy-release.sh itself, invoke the ARTIFACT'S OWN script\n   (`/opt/llm-manager/releases/<new-sha>/deploy/deploy-release.sh`) — the\n   current/deploy path is the OLD tooling until the new release activates, so\n   a new gate would silently not run (hit live 2026-09-29: the Phase-E dep\n   probe never executed because the old script was invoked; fixed by a\n   same-tip drill through the artifact's script). Phase E of the transaction\n   also now RUNS a dependency-import verification (window-4 PR #67): the\n   release venv must import sqlalchemy/alembic/psycopg2/httpx/uvicorn/fastapi\n   before cutover — missing dep = die = pre-activation abort + auto-rollback\n   (closes the window-3 sqlalchemy-gap class; tests/test_release_dep_gate.py).\n5. Poll `tail` of the log via ssh until `==> Release ACCEPTED (<full-sha>)`\n   + `healthcheck PASSED`. Note: `pgrep -f deploy-release` matches your OWN\n   ssh command line — filter with `pgrep -fl 'deploy-release.sh /tmp'` or\n   you'll read a phantom \"still running\".\n6. If `server_manager/` code changed: `systemctl restart server-manager-api`\n   (its own systemd unit; NOT restarted by the web-app transaction).\n   **Re-hit live 2026-10-01 (PR #74):** deploy-release.sh restarted\n   web/collector/emporia/recovery but NOT server-manager-api → the new SM\n   route 404'd via openapi until the manual restart. Make\n   `systemctl restart server-manager-api && curl -s :8300/openapi.json |\n   python3 -c \"…\"` the mandatory post-release verification whenever the diff\n   touches `server_manager/`.\n7. Check `deploy/ ops/` diffs before releasing: if the transaction tooling\n   itself changed, do a same-tip drill first (tooling-mid-flight is unproven).\n8. Verify: `curl -sk https://10.0.20.108/healthz` reports the new git_sha.\n   Clean the tarball+sha out of `/tmp` after success.\n9. **Env persistence across releases:** systemd DROP-INS survive releases —\n   env set via drop-in files (e.g.\n   `/etc/systemd/system/llm-manager-web.service.d/orm-cutover.conf`) is NOT\n   lost when a new release ships; plain env vars passed at restart time ARE.\n   There is no `/opt/llm-manager/.env` (staging toggle learned this: `touch \"\"\n`).\n\n## Staging VM120 (10.0.20.131) — access reality\n\n- NO direct SSH shell for the agent (no root ssh from workstation or VM114; the\n  CT130 self-hosted runner's `llm-manager-deploy@10.0.20.131` key is the ONLY\n  ssh path, and that user's sudo is wrapper-only: bootstrap/deploy/healthcheck\n  wrappers — arbitrary sudo asks for a password). **This is INTENTIONAL\n  least-privilege design, not a missing key (diagnosed 2026-09-29, window-4\n  plan §F1) — do not \"fix\" it by widening sudo.**\n- **qga IS available on VM120** (node miam-00135, `qm guest exec 120` — exit 0\n  verified). Window-3's \"no shell access\" finding was SSH-only; bounded\n  read-only verification (ORM shadow parity, DB counts, settings) works via\n  node-side qga exec with base64-staged scripts. CT130 lives on miam-00133;\n  its `llm-runner` user holds the llm-manager deploy key (`su - llm-runner`).\n- Staging auto-deploys on main push via `deploy-staging.yml`. Verify with\n  `curl -sk https://10.0.20.131/healthz` — the git_sha must match main tip.\n- One-shot env toggles go through the `staging-orm-cutover-toggle.yml`\n  workflow (workflow_dispatch, value=1/0) — it writes systemd drop-ins,\n  daemon-reloads, restarts, and verifies via `systemctl show`.\n- ORM shadow parity PROVEN on staging (window-4 Phase F3): slice-5\n  `orm_hosts_inventory()` == legacy psycopg2 host count; slice-6 gpu_orm\n  imports clean; retention settings 90/730 present; gpu tables empty on\n  staging by design (no GPU collectors target staging).\n- When VM120 access blocks a staging-first validation, prod validation via\n  VM114 root + systemd drop-ins is the accepted fallback (documented\n  deviation; prod cutover flag is instantly reversible by removing drop-ins).\n\n## CI / merge discipline\n\n- 6 required checks on PRs: secret-scan, syntax, tests, deps, config, db\n  (+ integration-smoke; `artifact` shows \"skipping\" on non-main-branch runs —\n  normal). Merge only when all pass:\n  `gh pr merge N --squash --delete-branch`.\n- Main pushes trigger `deploy-staging` → VM120 auto-deploy. Verify staging\n  picked up the SHA: `curl -sk https://10.0.20.131/healthz`.\n- **Admin-bypass pitfall:** Jordan's token has admin rights; branch protection\n  does NOT block `git push origin HEAD` from a local `main`. After a squash\n  merge you are left ON main — always `git checkout -b feat/...` BEFORE the\n  next commit, or you bypass review (happened twice 2026-09-28; CI covered it,\n  but it's against repo policy). **Confirmed still live 2026-09-29 (2 more\n  violations): the merge command itself leaves the local checkout ON main —\n  make `git branch --show-current` the mandatory first command after ANY\n  merge-with-delete.**\n- **CD artifact chain (3 bugs found + fixed 2026-09-29, PRs #61-#64):**\n  deploy-staging \"succeeded\" while deploying a 2-day-old artifact. (2) Do NOT `rm\n  artifact.zip` in the unpack step — the download step writes it fresh; rm-ing\n  it after download kills unzip (\"cannot find or open artifact.zip\"). (3)\n  `dist/` was TRACKED in the repo (no .gitignore entry) — the CI artifact zip\n  contained multiple committed stale tarballs; fixed by git-rm + gitignore +\n  a CI step asserting exactly ONE tarball pair before upload.\n  Verify staging health: `curl -sk https://10.0.20.131/healthz` must report\n  the current main git_sha — a \"successful\" deploy-staging run proves nothing\n  on its own.\n\n## PVE qga exec pattern (how you touch VM114/VM124)\n\n- Helper: `~/work/pve_api.py` (LLDAP bot account, `~/.pve_ldap_bot`). Node\n  locations: **VM114 + VM120 + CT122 on `miam-00135`**; **VM124 on\n  `miam00111`**; VM156 on `miam-00133`. Wrong node → 500 \"qemu-server/N.conf\n  does not exist\".\n- Two-step: `POST /nodes/{node}/qemu/{vmid}/agent/exec` (returns pid) → sleep\n  → `GET .../agent/exec-status?pid=<pid>` (out-data/err-data/exitcode).\n- Stage scripts as base64 (≤9KB chunks) written to a temp file, then decoded\n  and run. VM114 qga has a wedge risk on long execs — keep each exec\n  minimal, use nohup + log-poll for long operations.\n- **VM114 has root SSH too** (`ssh vm114` alias via `~/.ssh/config.d/`, dedicated\n  key) — simpler than qga for file staging (`scp` tarballs/probes to /tmp) and\n  log polling. Deploy-flow exceptions where qga/node-SSH is still required:\n  see `vm114-qga-ssh-recovery` skill.\n\n## ARM reconciler semantics (services/reconciliation.py)\n\n- desired RUNNING: observe → start owned VM once (ownership-verified, bounded\n  retries w/ capped backoff, `recovery_count`) → RUNNING; PVE truth corrects\n  stale DB actuals.\n- desired STOPPED: graceful shutdown once → **bounded convergence wait**\n  (`ARM_RECONCILE_SHUTDOWN_WAIT_S`, default 30s/3s polls). Live lesson: PVE\n  state flips LAG the API ack — an immediate status read still says `running`\n  after an accepted shutdown. Never record nonconvergence without the wait.\n- DESIRED_DESTROYED is API-only; the reconciler NEVER destroys. PVE\n  unreachable → record `last_error`, no state flip on unknown ground truth.\n- Single-replica discipline (TDR-0009): one uvicorn worker; no leader election\n  yet. state-sync health vocabulary: PENDING|SYNCING|VERIFIED|STALE|FAILED.\n- Stale failed-provisioning rows: disposition is **SUPERSEDED** (never delete).\n  Admin endpoint marks each failed attempt row SUPERSEDED and links it to the\n  live runtime (5 ERROR rows → live VM124 runtime 56c1b849, 2026-09-28, PR\n  #50). Backoff 10/20/40/60s capped\n  (attempts vary). Every attempt audits `failure_class` /\n  `recovery_method` / `attempt` in the RuntimeEvent detail (durable audit log\n  IS the recovery-method history — no second mutable table). Exhaustion\n  audits `strategy_next: human_attention`. Ladder: recheck/observe → VM start\n  ×5 → Human Attention. token usage from LiteLLM_SpendLogs (read-only, §10).\"\"\"\n    require_identity(request, \"usage:read\")\n    if hours < 1 or hours > 24 * 30:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, {"status": "success", "output": "\"\"\"Scoped service authentication (plan §11).\n\nMVP: scoped bearer credentials from a root-owned 0600 file, private network,\naudited, rotatable outside Git. Token file format:\n\n    # service   = <token>\n    svc-acms    = <token>\n    svc-server-manager = <token>\n\nEach caller identity carries an action scope. Documented TDR: temporary\nservice token → future mTLS (plan §11/§19).\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport hmac\nimport os\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom functools import lru_cache\n\nfrom fastapi import HTTPException, Request\n\n# Scope names (least-privilege defaults per §10B/§13)\nSCOPES_READ = {\"runtime:read\", \"job:read\", \"route:read\", \"usage:read\", \"health:read\"}\nSCOPES_WRITE = {\"runtime:write\", \"runtime:destroy\", \"job:submit\"}\nALL_SCOPES = SCOPES_READ | SCOPES_WRITE\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass ServiceIdentity:\n    service: str\n    scopes: frozenset[str]\n\n    def can(self, scope: str) -> bool:\n        return scope in self.scopes\n\n\ndef _load_identities() -> dict[str, tuple[str, frozenset[str]]]:\n    \"\"\"Returns {bearer_token: (service, scopes)} from the tokens file.\n\n    File format (0600, outside Git):\n        # service=token:scope1,scope2\n        svc-acms=token1:runtime:read,runtime:write,job:read,job:submit\n        svc-server-manager=token2:ALL\n    \"\"\"\n    path = os.environ.get(\"SERVER_MANAGER_SERVICE_TOKENS_FILE\", \"/etc/llm-manager/secrets/service_tokens\")\n    out: dict[str, tuple[str, frozenset[str]]] = {}\n    try:\n        with open(path) as f:\n            for line in f:\n                line = line.strip()\n                if not line or line.startswith(\"#\") or \"=\" not in line:\n                    continue\n                service, rest = line.split(\"=\", 1)\n                token, _, scope_s = rest.partition(\":\")\n                token = token.strip()\n                if not token:\n                    continue\n                if scope_s.strip() in (\"\", \"ALL\"):\n                    scopes = frozenset(ALL_SCOPES)\n                else:\n                    scopes = frozenset(x.strip() for x in scope_s.split(\",\") if x.strip())\n                out[token] = (service.strip(), scopes)\n    except OSError:\n        pass\n    return out\n\n\n@lru_cache(maxsize=1)\ndef _identities_cached(mtime: float) -> dict[str, tuple[str, frozenset[str]]]:\n    return _load_identities()\n\n\ndef _identities() -> dict[str, tuple[str, frozenset[str]]]:\n    path = os.environ.get(\"SERVER_MANAGER_SERVICE_TOKENS_FILE\", \"/etc/llm-manager/secrets/service_tokens\")\n    try:\n        mtime = os.stat(path).st_mtime\n    except OSError:\n        mtime = 0\n    return _identities_cached(mtime)\n---agent-runtimes routes---\n", "tool_calls_made": 1, "duration_seconds": 0.03}

{"status": "success", "output": "return _identities_cached(mtime)\n\n\ndef constant_time_lookup(token: str) -> ServiceIdentity | None:\n    identities = _identities()\n    for tok, (service, scopes) in identities.items():\n        if hmac.compare_digest(tok, token):\n            return ServiceIdentity(service=service, scopes=scopes)\n    return None\n\n\ndef require_identity(request: Request, scope: str) -> ServiceIdentity:\n    \"\"\"FastAPI dependency: verify bearer token + scope; 401 first, then 403.\"\"\"\n    auth = request.headers.get(\"Authorization\", \"\")\n    if not auth.startswith(\"Bearer \"):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail=\"missing bearer credential\")\n    token = auth[len(\"Bearer \"):].strip()\n    ident = constant_time_lookup(token)\n    if ident is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail=\"invalid credential\")\n    if not ident.can(scope):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=f\"identity '{ident.service}' lacks scope '{scope}'\")\n    return ident\n---modelpolicy---\n", "tool_calls_made": 1, "duration_seconds": 0.03}

{"status": "success", "output": "async def _find_existing_task(db: AsyncSession, dedupe_key: str) -> Any | None:\n    \"\"\"The most recent task created from this dedupe key.\n\n    The dedupe ledger lives in the durable EXECUTION_DISPATCHED event metadata\n    (``dedupe_key`` → ``task_id``) — external_task_id now carries the A2A run\n    id (ADR-0012 callback binding), so it can no longer double as the idem-\n    potency ledger. The event log is the same durability class ACMS already\n    relies on for reassignment counting (PR #32 pattern).\n    \"\"\"\n    rows = (await db.scalars(\n        select(AgentEventRecord)\n        .where(AgentEventRecord.event_type == \"EXECUTION_DISPATCHED\")\n        .order_by(AgentEventRecord.timestamp.desc())\n        .limit(200)\n    )).all()\n    for ev in rows:  # newest first\n        try:\n            meta = json.loads(ev.metadata_json) if ev.metadata_json else {}\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n            continue\n        if meta.get(\"dedupe_key\") == dedupe_key and meta.get(\"task_id\"):\n            return await db.get(ExecutionTaskRecord, meta[\"task_id\"])\n    return None\n\n\nasync def dispatch_work(db: AsyncSession, *, work_item_id: str,\n                        instruction: str, agent_id: str | None = None,\n                        idempotency_key: str | None = None,\n                        session_id: str | None = None,\n                        actor: str = \"api\") -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Dispatch NEW cloud execution for a Work Item — the ONLY path that does.\n\n    Returns a dict:\n      dispatched: bool          — False when the gate blocked or dedupe hit\n      reason: str               — dispatched | hard_budget_exceeded | duplicate\n      task_id / external_task_id — correlation ids\n      budget_check: dict        — the verdict used for the gate (audit trail)\n    Raises DispatchError for structural failures (unknown work/agent/bridge).\n    \"\"\"\n    # ---- 1. resolve the work item (+ open assignment) ----------------------\n    work = await db.get(WorkItemRecord, work_item_id)\n    if work is None:\n        raise DispatchError(\"unknown-work-item\", 404)\n    assignments = await work_service.list_assignments(db, work_item_id=work_item_id)\n    active = next((a for a in assignments if a.status == \"ACTIVE\"), None)\n    if agent_id is None and active is None:\n        raise DispatchError(\"no-active-assignment\", 409)\n    target_agent = agent_id or active.agent_id\n    if active is not None and agent_id is not None and agent_id != active.agent_id:\n        raise DispatchError(\"agent-mismatch\", 409,\n                            {\"active_assignment_agent\": active.agent_id})\n\n    # ---- 2. idempotency: a retry must never double-dispatch ----------------\n    dedupe_key = _idempotency_key(work_item_id, active.assignment_id if active else None,\n                                  idempotency_key)\n    existing = await _find_existing_task(db, dedupe_key)\n    if existing is not None and existing.status == \"RUNNING\":\n        return {\"dispatched\": False, \"reason\": \"duplicate\",\n                \"task_id\": existing.task_id, \"external_task_id\": None,\n                \"budget_check\": None}\n", "tool_calls_made": 1, "duration_seconds": 0.03}

JiraClient is fixture-mocked)\n    lr = client.post(f\"/api/v1/jira/work/{w}/link\", headers=AUTH,\n                     json={\"issue_key\": \"STNA-100\"})\n    assert lr.status_code == 200, lr.text\n    a = client.post(\"/api/v1/work/assignments\", headers=AUTH,\n                    json={\"work_item_id\": w, \"agent_id\": agent_id})\n    assert a.status_code in (200, 201), a.text\n    return w\n\n\ndef _set_budget(client, work_id, soft=None, hard=None):\n    payload = {}\n    if soft is not None:\n        payload[\"soft_budget_usd\"] = soft\n    if hard is not None:\n        payload[\"hard_budget_usd\"] = hard\n    r = client.put(f\"/api/v1/budgets/work/{work_id}\", headers=AUTH, json=payload)\n    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text\n\n\ndef _mk_session(client, work_id):\n    \"\"\"A memory session for the work item (agent id resolved from assignment).\"\"\"\n    s = client.post(\"/api/v1/memory/sessions\", headers=AUTH, json={\n        \"agent_id\": _agent_id(client), \"work_item_id\": work_id}).json()\n    return s[\"session_id\"]\n\n\ndef _add_cost(client, work_id, cost):\n    sid = _mk_session(client, work_id)\n    if cost is not None:\n        client.post(f\"/api/v1/memory/sessions/{sid}/telemetry\",\n                    headers=AUTH, json={\"estimated_cost_usd\": cost})\n    client.post(f\"/api/v1/memory/sessions/{sid}/close\", headers=AUTH)\n\n\ndef _dispatch(client, work_id, **kw):\n    body = {\"instruction\": kw.pop(\"instruction\", \"do the thing\")}\n    body.update(kw)\n    return client.post(f\"/api/v1/dispatch/work/{work_id}\", headers=AUTH, json=body)\n\n\ndef _events(client, event_type):\n    from acms.db import engine as _eng  # noqa: F401 — ensures module import\n    import asyncio\n    from sqlalchemy import select\n    from acms.telemetry_models import AgentEventRecord\n    from acms.db import get_session  # noqa: F401\n\n    from acms.db import engine\n", "tool_calls_made": 1, "duration_seconds": 0.03}
